  • Expected iteration domain per cell: 1..1000.
  • Cell key: (rt_id, hypothesis).

  • Run-evidence comes from filenames and log-tail parsing in data/derived/5_idiosyncrasy_logs.
  • Optional manual notes are loaded from data/derived/idiosyncrasy_completeness_manual_comments.csv.
